{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_13","type":"mcp_tool_call","server":"codex_apps","tool":"linear_fetch","arguments":{"id":"issue:JSC-246"},"result":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"{\"id\":\"JSC-246\",\"title\":\"Build repo surface contract and agent capability control-plane golden paths\",\"description\":\"## Summary\\n\\nTurn the Agent Skills Kit from a skill-repo framing into an agent capability control plane with a repo surface ownership contract, non-destructive bloat inventory gate, and task-first `ask` golden paths.\\n\\n## Source spec\\n\\n`Docs/specs/2026-05-01-feat-agent-capability-control-plane-and-repo-surface-contract-spec.md`\\n\\n## Why\\n\\nThe repo has strong infrastructure: canonical skill source, generated command handles, runtime projection, sync, audits, runtime budget checks, workouts, artifacts, and deferred context. The problem is that source, generated state, historical evidence, runtime mirrors, archived material, and product workflows are not consistently separated at the filesystem or CLI level.\\n\\nThis creates friction for both humans and agents:\\n\\n* humans see too many concepts before they see the value\\n* agents must infer which surfaces are canonical, generated, archived, or safe to edit\\n* deadcode cleanup risks becoming deletion-by-taste instead of a repeatable validated operation\\n\\n## Required behavior\\n\\n* Define a repo surface ownership policy for source, fixture, policy, reference, intentional archive, vendored snapshot, generated output, runtime state, historical artifact, and unknown classifications.\\n* Add a non-destructive inventory gate routed through `./bin/ask` with stable JSON output and concise human output.\\n* Report tracked generated/historical artifacts under `artifacts/**` and `Infrastructure/artifacts/**` before deleting anything.\\n* Flag suspicious nested paths such as `Infrastructure/Infrastructure/**` unless explicitly allowlisted.\\n* Require explicit ownership decisions for `.skillsets/**`, `.harness/*.db`, `skills-system/**`, and `Plugins/cache/**`.\\n* Preserve deferred context behind indexed references instead of trimming important context.\\n* Add or specify task-first golden paths such as `ask doctor`, `ask onboard`, `ask improve`, `ask explain`, `ask prove`, `ask next --json`, and `ask closeout --changed`.\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n- [ ] SA1: Repo surface ownership policy exists and defines the required classifications.\\n- [ ] SA2: Surface inventory command reports tracked files by classification with JSON output.\\n- [ ] SA3: Inventory flags tracked generated artifacts under `artifacts/**` and `Infrastructure/artifacts/**` unless allowlisted.\\n- [ ] SA4: Inventory flags `Infrastructure/Infrastructure/**` unless allowlisted with a reason.\\n- [ ] SA5: `.skillsets/**`, `.harness/*.db`, and `skills-system/**` have explicit ownership decisions before cleanup touches them.\\n- [ ] SA6: Historical artifact cleanup preserves required fixtures and summaries while removing only unreferenced generated evidence.\\n- [ ] SA7: Retired skill debris is cleaned only after active route, deferred context, and docs references are scanned.\\n- [ ] SA8: Deferred context remains reachable through indexed references and is not loaded by default.\\n- [ ] SA9: `ask doctor` reports repo, sync, runtime budget, handles, surface policy, blockers, and next command.\\n- [ ] SA10: `ask onboard` explains repo/runtime state and recommends the next useful action.\\n- [ ] SA11: `ask improve \\\"<goal>\\\"` maps goals to candidate capabilities, overlap notes, and validation/sync actions.\\n- [ ] SA12: `ask explain <handle>` distinguishes generated command handle, canonical source, runtime projection, loaded references, and validation commands.\\n- [ ] SA13: `ask closeout --changed` infers changed canonical files and focused validation/sync needs.\\n- [ ] SA14: Runtime surface reporting remains part of closeout.\\n- [ ] SA15: Product framing presents Agent Skills Kit
